# 09 Rubric Writing Plan — task_inputsS2 (Robert: what each bet cost to build and brought in; injection SQL sha256 40f90385e31e)
Generated 2026-11-09 · Clock 2026-02-14T00:00:00Z (seed bound; eval clock 2026-02-14 00:00 UTC, Feb 14, 2026 (normal)) · Persona Robert robert@harmonygames.co · Assigned role Co-founder & Creative Director · Tier Long-Horizon · Mode Easy · Human time 14.25 h (05_Human_Time.md) · OE status: /oe PASS 2026-11-08 (plateform_reports/6aeed82596dcfe103f9f74d9_2_oe (1).md, Score 5, 61 events, reviewed 08_Oracle_Events.md sha256 80a38ea27cd4, matching the file on disk)
Canonical outcomes: brief §4b (all fair results and accepted alternatives) — a new native spreadsheet owned by Robert, in his My Drive root or a folder he owns, any title, in Leonard's layout (Payee, Name on the bank, Bucket, Domino Delights, ZM, Combo Fighter, Company, Note) with 22 payee lines ENG-2438 to ENG-2459, keyed on the account ending, Build and Marketing dollars by bet, Company dollars in the Company column, ENG-2454 at 1,999.00 BGN, ENG-2450 and ENG-2459 at zero, build cost lines 959,342.64 / 544,433.44 / 72,685.61, brought-in 12,331.25 (Domino Delights) and 11.99 (ZM) with Combo Fighter's left off, shared with leonard.hayes@harmonygames.co and arthur.blake@harmonygames.co; a reason comment on ENG-2439, ENG-2442, ENG-2447, ENG-2450 and ENG-2459 before each moves to Done, all 22 issues Done; then one reply to Leonard in #winddown C0ADGSZKR3R (top level or under either post) with what each bet cost and brought in, what changes on the draft deck, and the link.

Recorded decisions:

- Process rows P66–P72 stay Process: the method says 'say why on the issue first' and 'move each payee’s issue to Done once its line is right', and the prompt says 'When you’re done, reply'; the final comments, states and reply look the same whichever write came first, so no Outcome row can prove the order. §C records the three-condition test per row.
- Marketing dollars are graded in the bet columns: the method lists Marketing 'under its bet by date like the rest, but keep it out of build cost'. Brief §4b also accepts those dollars 'in the row with the bet named', so a bet-column row on a Marketing line that locks the column carries an overly-specific risk (see Findings in 09_Rubric_Check.md while one is open).
- Slot X9 widened to the Company cell or the Note (brief §4b, ledger §F risk 4); slot D4 widened to top level or a thread under either post (ledger §F risk 5); no row grades a Combo Fighter revenue figure beyond the held-back rows (NB-26).

Rubric shape: every write family has a result row. Per-line values on the sheet are direct where an authority or the method singles the line out (the adjusted lines and the exception buckets) and sampled in the populations (sheet volume, bucket column, dollar amounts, name on the bank), each with the sheet's volume row and no bundled exact-set row (gate 15). The 22 Done moves and the 5 reason comments are graded per member; no count row sits over separate writes (count-bundle-v1). 09_Rubrics.json mirrors P1–P73 one to one (C-n = P-n).

## E · Summary
Rows 73 · Outcome 66 · Process candidates 7 considered, 7 kept — ≥ 1 Outcome ☑ · Process rows ≤ 50 % ☑ · fewer than two Process rows failing the three-condition test ☑ (none fails; §C records all 7) · builder minimum 5 ☑ · one volume row per sampled output, no bundled exact-set row ☑ (P5) · predicted all-fail rows = 0 ☑ (every value is reachable through a cataloged tool and visible to Robert; none sits on the four Evals bases)
Failure diversity: largest unit of discovery response robert's mailbox, subject payment processed 98% of the weight (164 of 167) (≤ 50% ☒ — every line's value depends on the issue list, the method and the notices through the ledger's Depends-on edges, so it is upstream of almost every row by the task's design; recorded with the numbers and left as it is); next response ENG-2445 98% and response ENG-2442 98% · chains landing on distinct rows ☑ (C1 P7/P24/P31…, C2 P8/P24/P32…, C3 P6/P24/P30…, C4 P9/P10/P33, C5 P12/P73, C6 P25/P26/P58…)
Bands: 5 ×17 · 3 ×13 · 1 ×43 · total weight 167
Format 5 (§A3): rows 73/73 ☑ · `do_nothing_case` REJECT 73/73 ☑ · reachability `PROMPT_LINKED` 73/73 ☑ · final-reply test ACCEPT ☑
Set size: 73 rows for 14.25 h (reference: 31 criteria for a ~10-hour task). The count follows the deliverables: 5 sheet setup and share rows, 19 sheet-line rows, 6 build-cost and revenue rows, 5 reason comments, 22 Done moves, 9 reply rows and 7 order rows; the members are per-target writes and cells, not duplicates of one another.
Coverage: requirements 12/12 (ledger R1–R12) · OEs 61/61 (§B) · writes 6/6 (sheet created; payee lines; shares; reason comments; Done moves; #winddown reply) · told facts 0/0 (the final response is ungraded; the prompt's 'reply to him' is the Slack write, graded by the reply rows) · friction 0/0 (Friction: none)
Gradable surface (gate 17): slots 29 · covered 25 · dispositioned 4 (§G) · no slot with neither ☑ · mechanism share 41% of the weight (68 of 167, the rows that cover the 16 slots a mechanism can flip; 50% counting every row a mechanism's node decides, since rows.json maps one row per slot; reported, not gated)
Four prohibitions: ☑ unstated expectations (every row quotes a prompt span; Leonard's method doc and sheet, which the prompt says to follow, are the incorporated authority) ☑ conclusions on ambiguous evidence (none: each graded value is settled by a later or more specific record, and ledger §F's risks are recorded per row) ☑ grading the distractor (traps are graded through the correct value, never their disclosure) ☑ hard-coded titles/phrasings (no file name, column wording beyond Leonard's layout, or message wording is required)
Blocking: none for validity — no UGT defect, no infeasible write, no unverified visibility. diversity.authority_share is a structural finding reported to the requester (see Unresolved in the Self-review).

## Self-review

Re-read as /rubric: walked Evals/3_Rubrics_Eval.md 1.4 and 3.1 against the plan in order. Every prompt requirement (ledger R1–R12) and every write family (the new sheet in Robert's Drive and its two shares; the 22 payee lines with buckets, bet columns, the Company column, build costs and revenue; the five reason comments; the 22 Done moves; the #winddown reply with costs, revenue, deck changes and the link) has a result row on the correct artifact. The final response is ungraded: the prompt's 'reply to him in #winddown' is a Slack write, so a concise chat answer passes the whole set (§A3). Friction is none, so no recovery outcome is needed.

Re-verified, every value in this build (ledger values were leads, not evidence):

- Seeded values: all 5 distinct source records of 00_V3_Authoring_Evidence.json that cite the SQL re-read from a fresh parse of 01_Universe_Inject.sql (tools/base_check.py parse_sql, never executed): 5 of 5 hold their source_value in the named table, identity and column.
- Base records: 20 distinct records cited by the sidecar's base sources read through `python3 Task_Authoring/tools/hg.py search get-many`; 20 of 20 cited source values are held in the record read.
- Per-line and total values: 64 rows (2 bank names, 6 buckets, 6 build costs, 1 currency, 22 done moves, 8 figures, 10 reason comments, 6 revenue, 1 volume, 2 zero lines) equal solver/truth.json, the build costs recomputed from its Build lines (Domino Delights 959,342.64, ZM 544,433.44, Combo Fighter 72,685.61) and REVENUE_DAILY recomputed from the export (2,284 rows through `python3 Task_Authoring/tools/hg.py search get snowflake --file snowflake.json --pointer /tables/12/rows`, paged, summed with Decimal: combo_fighter 11.75, domino_delights 12,331.25, zombie_match_3d 11.99); 0 differences. The five reason-comment rows equal truth.json's reason_comment group; the Done rows are the 22 issues.
- Literals: 35 of 54 backticked literals found in the SQL text; `1,999.00 BGN`, `125.00` found in the base reads above; `1,000.00`, `11.75`, `11.99`, `12,331.25`, `13,975.00`, `16,428.57`, `28,128.00`, `31,250.00`, `337,594.00`, `4,000.00`, `46,201.50`, `530,736.14`, `544,433.44`, `72,685.61`, `959,342.64` are figures computed in solver/truth.json or the warehouse sums; `Own transfer`, `new spreadsheet` found in neither, each a label of the row's own (a state, a bucket word or a grading anchor, never a record value).

Count on the four bases: 0.

Shortcut and diversity: the §F probe's verdict is HITS (1100): 1100 HIT lines over 1100 records, 0 INCOMPLETE lines, and 1100 SHORTCUT-OK lines, one per record (628 generic-word hits, 434 address hits, 38 records read one by one). A generic-word or address hit holds only common words of the task or a founder's mail address and no rubric figure, id or bank name, so a run cannot answer from it. Every other hit is reasoned per record below: gmail robert@harmonygames.co/1768085436824934351 (1 figures or ids); gmail robert@harmonygames.co/1770004541443668339 (1 figures or ids); gmail robert@harmonygames.co/1795182296548841984 (1 figures or ids); gmail robert@harmonygames.co/1795182318659688694 (1 figures or ids). The largest holds 1 of the 56 values, so no record holds more than one payee's or one line's worth of the answer Largest unit of discovery: response slack messages naming node media, 164 of 167 (98%), then response the notices among the 132 of f31 whose body names account ending 1876 98% and response ENG-2457 98%. Every line's facts reach the issue list, the method doc and the notice population through the ledger's Depends-on edges (see Unresolved).

Surface dispositions: B4, S2, S3, S5 have no covering row and carry a §G disposition. Strongest case for a further row: B4 is a total, but the prompt and the method ask for a Company column and the OE's sheet write grades no Company total, so a Company-total row would be a count over separate items (count-bundle-v1, release b9281224) with no prompt span behind it (gate 3). S2 (Eveline Art Domino Delights), S3 (Graham Porter ZM), S5 (Trademark lawyer Company) are ordinary per-payee cells; each is a sum of notices with no adjustment of its own, the dollar-figure group already holds its five samples (gate 15), and the sheet volume row carries the rest. S6 is covered by the sheet's volume row, stated by issue range, because a Build-row count of nine would be a bare count over separate cells; D4 is covered by the reply content rows, each naming its #winddown reply, since a bare 'replied' row duplicates them.

Recorded decisions, re-checked against their sources in this build:

- Process rows P66–P72 stay Process: the method says 'say why on the issue first' and 'move each payee’s issue to Done once its line is right', and the prompt says 'When you’re done, reply'; the final comments, states and reply look the same whichever write came first, so no Outcome row can prove the order. §C records the three-condition test per row.
- Marketing dollars are graded in the bet columns: the method lists Marketing 'under its bet by date like the rest, but keep it out of build cost'. Brief §4b also accepts those dollars 'in the row with the bet named', so a bet-column row on a Marketing line that locks the column carries an overly-specific risk (see Findings in 09_Rubric_Check.md while one is open).
- Slot X9 widened to the Company cell or the Note (brief §4b, ledger §F risk 4); slot D4 widened to top level or a thread under either post (ledger §F risk 5); no row grades a Combo Fighter revenue figure beyond the held-back rows (NB-26).
